# Source Cleanup Inventory

Snapshot: 2026-11-07. Companion to [CLEANUP_MAP.md](CLEANUP_MAP.md).

Scope: all .js/.jsx/.ts/.tsx/.css files under src, including labs, archives, fixtures and tests. JSON data, public assets, scripts, dependencies and build output are excluded. Counts measure physical lines after trailing whitespace is trimmed; they are not executable-code counts.

A review status describes work in this chat only. Other agents may have reviewed additional files. Working changes are a snapshot, not an ownership claim. Recheck git status and coordinate before editing.

Importers counts distinct src files with resolvable relative static imports, re-exports, literal require or literal dynamic imports. It is NOT entry-point reachability or proof of live/dead code. It excludes nonliteral imports, external tooling and documentation references. CSS has no parsed outbound imports. Zero does not authorize deletion.

Files: 1,343. Lines: 180,468. Source parse failures: 0.

Review dates record the last inspection/refactor in this chat, at the depth stated in Review status. They do not imply a full audit. Active owner is a temporary edit claim, separate from historical reviewer; Unassigned does not mean the file is safe to edit without checking current work.
